<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.val.town/winding-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Val Town is winding down</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 即将停止运营</span></a><span class="dom">blog.val.town</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Val Town has decided to wind down its service. The team thanked users for trusting the platform and apologized that it could no longer continue serving them. The announcement does not provide further details about the shutdown timeline or transition.</span><span class='zh'>Val Town 决定逐步停止运营。团队感谢用户对平台的信任，并为无法继续提供服务表示歉意。公告没有进一步说明停运时间表或迁移安排。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='joshka.net' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Terminal borders that don&#x27;t cost a cell</span><span class='zh'>不占用终端单元格的边框</span></a><span class="dom">joshka.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns. The implementation works in Ghostty, with demos built using Ratatui. It aims to give terminal user interfaces more visual design options than conventional box-based layouts.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。该实现可在 Ghostty 中运行，并提供了使用 Ratatui 构建的演示。它旨在为终端用户界面提供比传统方框布局更多的视觉设计选择。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>404</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50491423' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>193</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50493062" data-id="50493062" data-tags="Programming|Hardware|Web|Code" data-score="399" data-comments="67" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493062" data-time="1794623309">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">65</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ggml v1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>ggml v1.0.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support. It also delivers performance work across CUDA, Metal, Vulkan, SYCL, OpenCL, WebGPU, and Hexagon backends, including quantized matrix multiplication, MoE fusion, FlashAttention paths, and scheduler improvements. The release broadens hardware and backend support while reducing unnecessary input uploads.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。该版本还改进了 CUDA、Metal、Vulkan、SYCL、OpenCL、WebGPU 和 Hexagon 后端的性能，包括量化矩阵乘法、MoE 融合、FlashAttention 路径和调度器优化。此次发布扩大了硬件与后端支持，并减少了不必要的输入上传。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool. Fifteen guests were injured as a result.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。结果造成 15 名客人受伤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reddit.com/r/Teachers/comments/1xuup7a/technology_sucks_it_literally_takes_16_clicks_for/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It Takes 16 Clicks for a Student to Open a PowerPoint on an iPad</span><span class='zh'>学生在 iPad 上打开一个 PowerPoint 要点 16 次点击</span></a><span class="dom">reddit.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/astronomers-found-a-mysterious-object-orbiting-far-beyond-pluto-it-could-be-a-new-dwarf-planet-hidden-in-our-solar-system-180989857/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dwarf planet candidate appears which takes roughly 280 years to orbit our sun</span><span class='zh'>一颗矮行星候选体出现了，绕太阳一周约需 280 年</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers identified a distant object called ED138 in telescope images from the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. It appears unusually large and would take roughly 280 years to orbit the Sun. Its size still needs to be determined before astronomers can classify it as a dwarf planet.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在维拉·鲁宾天文台的望远镜图像中发现了一个名为 ED138 的遥远天体。它看起来异常大，绕太阳一周大约需要 280 年。在将其归类为矮行星之前，天文学家仍需确定它的大小。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://gatezh.com/brand/share.381cc582d58a3410686473b92c1d944fbc83dc59a7e926952aeb6c5c0d8a6ef0.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='gatezh.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">69</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gatezh.com/posts/macos-secure-enclave-ssh-keys/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protecting SSH Keys with macOS Secure Enclave</span><span class='zh'>使用 macOS 安全隔区保护 SSH 密钥</span></a><span class="dom">gatezh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The proposed setup stores SSH keys in the macOS Secure Enclave and requires Touch ID for each use, without third-party applications. It is intended to prevent malicious software that can read the filesystem from simply copying private keys from ~/.ssh/. The approach is motivated in part by the risk of running untrusted code from fake interview assignments.</span><span class='zh'>这种方案将 SSH 密钥存储在 macOS 安全隔区中，并在每次使用时要求通过 Touch ID 验证，无需第三方应用。它旨在防止能够读取文件系统的恶意软件直接复制 ~/.ssh/ 中的私钥。作者提到，虚假面试作业要求用户克隆并运行代码的案例，也是采用这一方案的原因之一。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>382</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449748' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>136</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50487864" data-id="50487864" data-tags="Hardware|Business" data-score="382" data-comments="211" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487864" data-time="1794595244">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.freightwaves.com/news/11-4-million-in-nvidia-hardware-vanishes-during-shipment-from-california-to-texas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$11.4M in Nvidia hardware vanishes during shipment from CA to TX</span><span class='zh'>价值 1,140 万美元的英伟达硬件在从加州运往德州途中失踪</span></a><span class="dom">freightwaves.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Six pallets of NVIDIA computer hardware, reportedly worth $11.4 million, disappeared during shipment from Oakland, California, to Fort Worth, Texas. The truck was collected on August 29, made several extended stops, and was last tracked in San Bernardino County. Oakland police confirmed an ongoing grand-theft investigation, while the carrier’s identity and the circumstances remain unclear.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，六托盘英伟达计算机硬件在从加州奥克兰运往德州沃思堡途中失踪，价值 1,140 万美元。卡车于 8 月 29 日提货，途中多次长时间停留，最后在圣贝纳迪诺县失去踪迹。奥克兰警方已确认正在调查这起重大盗窃案，但承运方身份和货物失踪经过仍不清楚。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The discussion claims that Humble Bundle substantially weakened its security through “vibecoding.” The available description does not identify the affected systems, specific vulnerabilities, or the evidence behind the claim.</span><span class='zh'>这场讨论声称，Humble Bundle 通过“氛围编程”大幅削弱了自身的大部分安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The discussion claims that Humble Bundle substantially weakened its security through “vibecoding.” The available description does not identify the affected systems, specific vulnerabilities, or the evidence behind the claim.</span><span class='zh'>这场讨论声称，Humble Bundle 通过“氛围编程”大幅削弱了自身的大部分安全性。现有描述没有说明受影响的系统、具体漏洞或支持这一说法的证据。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree. It provides a visual representation of systemd jobs during system startup.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。它在系统启动过程中以可视化方式呈现 systemd 作业。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=pron.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>pron.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">73</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pron.github.io/posts/correctness-and-complexity" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing correct software is hard and math alone won’t help us (2016)</span><span class='zh'>写出正确的软件很难，光靠数学也无济于事（2016）</span></a><span class="dom">pron.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay examines why writing correct programs is inherently difficult. It reviews results from computability and complexity theory, arguing that complete understanding of a program—needed to establish correctness—is fundamentally at odds with the nature of programs. The piece expands a Curry On talk on computational complexity and software verification, with additional proofs and results.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章探讨了为什么编写正确的程序本质上很困难。文章回顾可计算性理论和复杂性理论的成果，认为要证明正确性所需的完整理解，与程序本身的性质存在根本冲突。文章扩展了作者在 Curry On 大会关于计算复杂性和软件验证的演讲，并加入了更多证明和结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>358</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50465352' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>169</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50478515" data-id="50478515" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="356" data-comments="217" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515" data-time="1794545693">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ice-wm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ice-wm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">74</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ice-wm.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceWM</span><span class='zh'>IceWM</span></a><span class="dom">ice-wm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceWM is a fast, simple, and highly configurable window manager for the X Window System. It provides a taskbar, keyboard and mouse controls, dynamic menus, multiple-monitor support through RandR and Xinerama, themes, and optional session and wallpaper managers. Created by Marko Maček in 1997 using C++, it is now maintained on GitHub and is available on many Linux distributions and BSD systems.</span><span class='zh'>IceWM 是面向 X Window System 的快速、简洁且高度可配置的窗口管理器。它提供任务栏、键盘和鼠标控制、动态菜单，以及通过 RandR 和 Xinerama 实现的多显示器支持，还支持主题和可选的会话、壁纸管理器。IceWM 由 Marko Maček 于 1997 年使用 C++ 创建，目前在 GitHub 上维护，并可用于多种 Linux 发行版和 BSD 系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>356</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50478515' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>217</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50462177" data-id="50462177" data-tags="Security" data-score="355" data-comments="191" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50462177" data-time="1794449471">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1794425853-threat-update-kics-alert_custom.jpeg?fm=webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wiz.io' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">75</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/cve-2026-119618-rce-apache-http-server-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Critical RCE Vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server</span><span class='zh'>Apache HTTP Server 存在严重远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-119618 is a critical race-condition vulnerability in Apache HTTP Server’s HTTP/2 module, mod_http2. The flaw affects the assign_headers() function when it handles shared resources, and a remote unauthenticated attacker could use it to corrupt heap memory and execute arbitrary code. Wiz reports that 64% of cloud environments have vulnerable resources; no public exploit or in-the-wild exploitation had been reported at publication time.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-119618 是 Apache HTTP Server HTTP/2 模块 mod_http2 中一个严重的竞态条件漏洞。该漏洞出现在 assign_headers() 函数处理共享资源时，未经身份验证的远程攻击者可能借此破坏堆内存并执行任意代码。Wiz 报告称，64% 的云环境存在易受攻击的资源；截至发布时，尚无公开漏洞利用程序或野外攻击报告。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code and Codex were used to reverse engineer the ECU of a 1994 Jeep. The project used a $3 Arduino as part of the setup. It is presented as a video demonstration of AI-assisted automotive reverse engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 和 Codex 被用于逆向工程一辆 1994 年 Jeep 的 ECU。该项目使用了一块 3 美元的 Arduino 作为设备的一部分。相关成果以视频形式展示了 AI 辅助的汽车系统逆向工程过程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edge0.ai/blog/introducing-edge0-145b-frontier-ai-capabilities-on-your-own-computer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Edge0-145B: Frontier AI Capabilities on Your Own Computer</span><span class='zh'>Edge0-145B：在自己的电脑上运行前沿 AI 能力</span></a><span class="dom">edge0.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edge0 has open-sourced Edge0-145B and Edge0-120B, along with an inference framework and deployment tools. Edge0-145B is described as an open-source, on-device model with 145 billion parameters for coding, reasoning, and agent tasks. Local inference avoids cloud-model token fees, but large models still require substantial memory, bandwidth, and power.</span><span class='zh'>Edge0 开源了 Edge0-145B 和 Edge0-120B，同时发布推理框架和部署工具。Edge0-145B 被称为首个开源的 1450 亿参数设备端模型，可用于编程、推理和智能体任务。本地推理无需支付云模型的 token 费用，但大型模型仍需要大量内存、带宽和电力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/tech/1012372/tv-privacy-acr-questions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I asked every TV manufacturer 17 questions about how they spy on you</span><span class='zh'>我向每家电视制造商提出了 17 个关于其如何监视用户的问题</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The investigation sent 17 yes-or-no privacy questions to 11 TV and streaming-device companies, but only a few answered every question. The questions covered issues such as recording or transcribing audio, taking screenshots, storing sensitive information, and sharing data with the cloud. The effort followed accusations involving LG and was intended to obtain clearer answers than lengthy privacy policies provide.</span><span class='zh'>这项调查向 11 家电视和流媒体设备公司提出了 17 个是非题式的隐私问题，但只有少数公司回答了全部问题。问题涉及录音或保存音频转录、截取屏幕、在电视上存储敏感信息，以及与云端共享数据等事项。调查起因之一是针对 LG 的监视指控，目的是获得比冗长隐私政策更清晰的回答。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50453474' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>237</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50503255" data-id="50503255" data-tags="Privacy|Legal" data-score="338" data-comments="122" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255" data-time="1794708054">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/siren_v._san_jose.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">79</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/11/were-asking-appeals-court-require-warrant-alpr-searches" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re Asking an Appeals Court to Require a Warrant for ALPR Searches</span><span class='zh'>我们请求上诉法院要求 ALPR 搜索取得搜查令</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The request asks an appeals court to require a warrant for searches using automated license plate readers. The argument is that ALPR systems can let law enforcement review a person&#x27;s past movements, rather than merely observe one license plate on a passing car. Their ability to search historical location records is therefore presented as materially different from ordinary roadside observation.</span><span class='zh'>该请求要求上诉法院规定，使用自动车牌识别系统进行搜索必须取得搜查令。其理由是，ALPR 能让执法部门查看某人的历史移动轨迹，而不仅是观察一辆经过车辆的单个车牌。因而，搜索历史位置记录的能力被认为与普通路边观察存在实质差异。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>338</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503255' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>122</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50451903" data-id="50451903" data-tags="Hardware|Security" data-score="336" data-comments="131" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903" data-time="1794394120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.kate.land/images/ga100-block-diagram.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.kate.land' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">80</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.kate.land/posts/using-voltage-glitching-to-unlock-the-a100/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using voltage glitching to unleash the A100 in my mining card</span><span class='zh'>通过电压故障注入解锁挖矿卡中的 A100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.kate.land</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A voltage fault-injection vulnerability in the GA100 die can reportedly blow arbitrary fuses on an NVIDIA CMP 170HX. The author used it to turn an 8GB CMP 170HX into a card functionally similar to an A100-80G, enabling roughly 120–126 functional SMs instead of the 108 used in production A100s. The method can also unlock features including NVLink, PCIe Gen4, NVJPG, NVDEC, and MIG, although several of those features were not specifically tested.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GA100 芯片中的电压故障注入漏洞可以烧断任意熔丝。作者利用这一点，将一张 8GB 的 NVIDIA CMP 170HX 改造成在功能上类似 A100-80G 的显卡，并启用了约 120 至 126 个可用 SM，而量产 A100 使用的是 108 个。该方法还可以解锁 NVLink、PCIe Gen4、NVJPG、NVDEC 和 MIG 等功能，但其中几项尚未经过专门测试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>336</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50451903' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>131</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50497009" data-id="50497009" data-tags="Privacy|Legal" data-score="336" data-comments="223" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497009" data-time="1794664086">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/italy-fines-meta-887-million-over-android-users-data-access-2026-11-14/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Italy fines Meta $887M over Android users&#x27; data access</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 罚款 8.87 亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Italy fined Meta $887 million over Android users’ data access.</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 处以 8.87 亿美元罚款。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Italy fined Meta $887 million over Android users’ data access. The case concerns Meta’s handling of data access involving users of Android devices.</span><span class='zh'>意大利因 Android 用户数据访问问题对 Meta 处以 8.87 亿美元罚款。案件涉及 Meta 如何处理 Android 设备用户的数据访问。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://research.meta.ai/blog/muse-ember" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://research.meta.ai/articles/muse-glint/hero/blog-header-light-v1.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='research.meta.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.meta.ai/blog/muse-ember" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muse Ember: Our smallest, most efficient model</span><span class='zh'>Muse Ember：我们最小、最高效的模型</span></a><span class="dom">research.meta.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta introduced Muse Ember as its smallest and most efficient model for complex reasoning execution and repetitive agent work.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 发布了 Muse Ember，称其为用于复杂推理执行和重复性智能体工作的最小、最高效模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta introduced Muse Ember as its smallest and most efficient model for complex reasoning execution and repetitive agent work. The target workloads include tool calls, coding subtasks, routing, extraction, and long-document parsing. Meta says it matches Claude Haiku 5.5&#x27;s best score at about one-third the cost and beats every listed GPT-6 Luna setting.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 发布了 Muse Ember，称其为用于复杂推理执行和重复性智能体工作的最小、最高效模型。目标工作负载包括工具调用、编程子任务、路由、信息抽取和长文档解析。Meta 称，该模型以约三分之一的成本达到 Claude Haiku 5.5 的最佳成绩，并超过所列出的 GPT-6 Luna 各项设置。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>330</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429024' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>140</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50446246" data-id="50446246" data-tags="Education|Science" data-score="328" data-comments="58" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50446246" data-time="1794344324">
<a class="thumb" href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/six-math-essentials.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=teorth.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>teorth.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">83</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/six-math-essentials.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Six Math Essentials</span><span class='zh'>Terence Tao 的六个数学要点</span></a><span class="dom">teorth.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In Six Math Essentials, Fields Medalist Terence Tao introduces six concepts that span mathematical thinking from antiquity to the modern frontier. The book covers numbers, algebra, geometry, probability, analysis, and dynamics as gateways to quantitative thought, abstraction, calculation, uncertainty, scale, and change. No errata have been recorded for the book.</span><span class='zh'>菲尔兹奖得主 Terence Tao 在《六个数学要点》中介绍了贯穿古代至现代数学前沿的六个核心概念。全书分别以数字、代数、几何、概率、分析和动力学为入口，讨论定量思维、抽象、计算、不确定性、尺度与变化。目前该书尚无勘误记录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hugovk.dev/blog/2026/lock-github-actions/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lock Your GitHub Actions</span><span class='zh'>锁定你的 GitHub Actions</span></a><span class="dom">hugovk.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Actions now supports workflow lockfiles. Previously, users were advised to pin actions to a full Git commit SHA because tags can be changed and redirected to malicious code in supply-chain attacks. Lockfiles provide another mechanism for constraining the actions used by a workflow.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Actions 现在支持工作流锁定文件。此前，建议用户将 action 固定到完整的 Git 提交 SHA，因为标签可以被修改，攻击者可能借此在供应链攻击中将原本安全的标签指向恶意代码。锁定文件为限制工作流所使用的 actions 提供了另一种机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://projectzero.google/2026/11/disclosure-policy.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Project Zero will disclose vulnerabilities 90 days after reporting</span><span class='zh'>Project Zero 将在报告漏洞 90 天后公开信息</span></a><span class="dom">projectzero.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Since 2021, Project Zero has followed a 90-plus-30-day disclosure policy, giving vendors 90 days to remediate a vulnerability and an additional 30 days for patch rollout.</span><span class='zh'>自 2021 年起，Project Zero 采用“90+30 天”披露政策，给予厂商 90 天修复漏洞，另加 30 天用于推广补丁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Since 2021, Project Zero has followed a 90-plus-30-day disclosure policy, giving vendors 90 days to remediate a vulnerability and an additional 30 days for patch rollout. It says AI-assisted vulnerability discovery and exploitation have increased the immediate risk posed by newly reported bugs. Project Zero is therefore changing its policy to encourage faster remediation and disclosure after 90 days.</span><span class='zh'>自 2021 年起，Project Zero 采用“90+30 天”披露政策，给予厂商 90 天修复漏洞，另加 30 天用于推广补丁。该团队表示，AI 辅助的漏洞发现与利用提高了新报告漏洞的即时风险。因此，Project Zero 将调整政策，以推动更快修复，并在报告 90 天后披露漏洞信息。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://haddyalchaer.com/jepa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>After 600 experiments, here&#x27;s what I discovered about JEPA world models</span><span class='zh'>经过 600 次实验，我对 JEPA 世界模型有了这些发现</span></a><span class="dom">haddyalchaer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author reports conducting 600 experiments on JEPA world models.</span><span class='zh'>作者报告称围绕 JEPA 世界模型进行了 600 次实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author reports conducting 600 experiments on JEPA world models. The work is framed against a debate over whether world models should generate future pixels or instead learn representations focused on information useful for decision-making. Supporters of the latter view, including Yann LeCun, argue that many pixels are irrelevant or inherently unpredictable for planning long-horizon tasks.</span><span class='zh'>作者报告称围绕 JEPA 世界模型进行了 600 次实验。相关工作置于一场争论之中：世界模型究竟应生成未来像素，还是应学习聚焦于决策有用信息的表示。包括 Yann LeCun 在内的支持者认为，许多像素与规划无关，或本身就无法被准确预测。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>288</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487185' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>48</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50454625" data-id="50454625" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="285" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625" data-time="1794411186">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings. It runs in the background without joining calls, and keeps transcripts, recordings, and summaries on the user’s computer. The app also offers calendar integration and a local MCP server for optionally sharing notes with external frontier models.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。它在后台运行，不会加入通话，会议转录、录音和摘要也不会离开用户的电脑。该应用还提供日历集成和本地 MCP 服务器，用户可以选择通过它将笔记分享给外部前沿模型。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude is launching Whiteboards and Sheets in beta, while Claude Dashboards becomes generally available as Claude Data.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 推出了 Whiteboards 和 Sheets 测试版，同时将 Claude Dashboards 正式更名并开放为 Claude Data。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude is launching Whiteboards and Sheets in beta, while Claude Dashboards becomes generally available as Claude Data. Whiteboards supports shared brainstorming, Sheets lets Claude build and edit spreadsheets, and Claude Data provides data stories, certified queries, and live sharing. All three are available on paid plans and keep documents, boards, sheets, and dashboards inside Claude for team collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 推出了 Whiteboards 和 Sheets 测试版，同时将 Claude Dashboards 正式更名并开放为 Claude Data。Whiteboards 支持协作头脑风暴，Sheets 可由 Claude 创建和编辑电子表格，Claude Data 提供数据故事、认证查询和实时共享。三项功能均面向付费计划，并允许团队在 Claude 内继续协作处理文档、白板、表格和仪表板。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ign.com/articles/nintendo-takes-down-switch-emulators-and-mod-chip-firmware-in-massive-github-dmca-sweep" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nintendo Takes Down Emulators and Firmware in Massive GitHub DMCA Sweep</span><span class='zh'>任天堂在 GitHub 发起大规模 DMCA 清理，移除模拟器和固件</span></a><span class="dom">ign.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nintendo sent four DMCA takedown notices to GitHub targeting the Yuzu and Citron Neo Switch emulators and mod-chip firmware.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂向 GitHub 发出四份 DMCA 删除通知，目标包括 Yuzu 和 Citron Neo Switch 模拟器，以及改装芯片固件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nintendo sent four DMCA takedown notices to GitHub targeting the Yuzu and Citron Neo Switch emulators and mod-chip firmware. GitHub removed 144 repositories containing the firmware and replaced them with DMCA notices. The firmware can be flashed to mod chips such as Picofly, which are used to circumvent the Switch’s copy-protection mechanisms.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂向 GitHub 发出四份 DMCA 删除通知，目标包括 Yuzu 和 Citron Neo Switch 模拟器，以及改装芯片固件。GitHub 已移除包含该固件的 144 个仓库，并以 DMCA 通知页面取代。相关固件可刷入 Picofly 等改装芯片，用于绕过 Switch 的版权保护机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.pypi.org/posts/2026-11-06-ui-updates-complete/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PyPI&#x27;s redesigned project pages with clearer security signals are now live</span><span class='zh'>PyPI 重设计的项目页面现已上线，安全信号更加清晰</span></a><span class="dom">blog.pypi.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PyPI has completed and launched its redesigned project pages.</span><span class='zh'>PyPI 已完成并上线重新设计的项目页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PyPI has completed and launched its redesigned project pages. The new interface adds a dedicated security tab, a rebuilt release-files tab, and refreshed documentation to make package details and release-file provenance easier to inspect. The changes were shaped by user research with members of the Python community.</span><span class='zh'>PyPI 已完成并上线重新设计的项目页面。新界面增加了专门的安全标签页、重做的发布文件标签页和更新后的文档，让用户更容易查看软件包详情及发布文件的来源。这些改动参考了对 Python 社区成员开展的用户研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vaibhaav-Tiwari/deepseek-v3-jax" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DeepSeek V3 in ~200 lines of pure Jax</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用约 200 行纯 JAX 实现 DeepSeek V3</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction. The model was pretrained from scratch on a MacBook Air using public-domain detective fiction, without PyTorch, Flax, or Equinox. Its parameters are plain dictionaries of arrays, and the implementation is organized as readable functions.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。该模型在 MacBook Air 上使用公版侦探小说从头预训练，未使用 PyTorch、Flax 或 Equinox。模型参数采用由数组组成的普通字典，代码则组织为可从头读到尾的函数。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Process Explorer for macOS</span><span class='zh'>适用于 macOS 的 Process Explorer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience. It provides a live process tree, module, handle, and thread inspection, per-process properties, system-wide performance graphs, and code-signing checks. The Swift, SwiftUI, and AppKit application uses public macOS APIs, with optional support for a privileged helper.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。它提供实时进程树、模块、句柄和线程检查、逐进程属性、系统级性能图表以及代码签名检查。该应用使用 Swift、SwiftUI 和 AppKit 构建，并通过 macOS 公共 API 工作，还可选择使用特权辅助程序。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/datareceipts/data-receipts/tree/main/109-gpu-pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The same H100 costs $2.69 or $12.29 an hour depending where you rent it</span><span class='zh'>同一块 H100 的租用价格可能是每小时 2.69 或 12.29 美元</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour. For identical GPUs, it reports a 4.57× gap between the cheapest and most expensive options. Running one GPU continuously for a month costs $1,964 or $8,972 depending only on the provider.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。对于相同 GPU，最便宜和最昂贵选项之间的价格差达到 4.57 倍。连续运行一块 GPU 一个月，费用可能因服务商不同而为 1964 美元或 8972 美元。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3 – A standalone XMPP server written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3：用 Rust 编写的独立 XMPP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features. It remains a pre-1.0, single-node server, while full XMPP compliance and clustering are still on the roadmap. The release expands secure federation without yet providing those larger deployment features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。它仍是一个处于 1.0 之前阶段的单节点服务器，完整 XMPP 兼容性和集群支持仍在路线图中。此次更新增强了安全联邦能力，但尚未提供这些更大规模部署功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/IRS-Public/direct-file" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US IRS free online tax filing open source repo (2025)</span><span class='zh'>美国国税局免费在线报税开源仓库（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS. It uses interview-style questions, works across phones and computers, supports English and Spanish, and translates users&#x27; answers into tax forms submitted through the IRS Modernized e-File API. The repository contains the service&#x27;s code and related tests and scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。该服务采用问答式界面，可在手机和电脑等设备上使用，支持英语和西班牙语，并将用户答案转换为税表后通过国税局 Modernized e-File API 提交。该开源仓库包含服务代码以及相关测试和场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>148</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499083' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>91</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460154" data-id="50460154" data-tags="Hardware|Programming" data-score="140" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154" data-time="1794438158">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tylertms/Stela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A general purpose Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs</span><span class='zh'>面向 x86-64 PC 的通用 Apple Silicon 层</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs. It includes an Arm64-to-x86-64 translator, an NVIDIA Metal driver, and a model of Apple silicon hardware, and can currently boot macOS and most apps. The v0.1.0 release is slow, requires more testing, and has a low chance of booting on current hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。它包含 Arm64 到 x86-64 的转换器、NVIDIA Metal 驱动以及 Apple 芯片硬件模型，目前可以启动 macOS 和大多数应用。v0.1.0 运行速度较慢，仍需大量测试，在当前硬件上成功启动的概率也很低。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>140</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50449891" data-id="50449891" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI" data-score="138" data-comments="60" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891" data-time="1794373007">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adrbn/bo2-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Black Ops II statically recompiled to native ARM64 macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator. The game reportedly runs at nearly 60 frames per second across the campaign, local multiplayer with bots, and local Zombies mode, including owned DLC maps. Split-screen is being worked on, while LAN mode is planned for later.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。据称，战役、本地机器人多人模式和本地僵尸模式都能以接近 60 帧运行，已拥有的 DLC 地图也可使用。开发者正在处理分屏功能，局域网模式则计划稍后加入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/snipedbywifi/terminactory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A terminal-native idle factory game, written in Rust with ratatui</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 和 ratatui 编写的终端原生放置工厂游戏</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks. It includes basic factory mechanics such as building, deleting, selecting, measuring earnings, and researching unlocks. Additional features include a fictional browser with a wiki and forums, extensive lore, and hooks that display coding-agent responses as in-game “telegrams.”</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。游戏包含建造、删除、选择、收益测算和研究解锁等基本工厂机制。它还加入了带有 Wiki 和论坛的虚构浏览器、大量背景故事，以及将编码代理回复显示为游戏内“电报”的钩子功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/MACaQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Huawei releases MACaQ, its internal quantizer for local inference</span><span class='zh'>华为发布用于本地推理的内部量化器 MACaQ</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models. It supports llama.cpp, vLLM, Diffusers, and PyTorch. Huawei says it can quantize a 122-billion-parameter model in 64 minutes using 32.5 GB of device memory, while achieving lower perplexity than MR-GPTQ and DuQuant++ in stated comparisons.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。它兼容 llama.cpp、vLLM、Diffusers 和 PyTorch。华为称，MACaQ 可使用 32.5 GB 设备内存，在 64 分钟内完成一个 1220 亿参数模型的量化；在其给出的比较中，困惑度低于 MR-GPTQ 和 DuQuant++。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kurtzer&#x27;s Ginux is now a userspace kernel</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kurtzer 的 Ginux 现已成为用户态内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances. It runs as an ordinary Linux process and defines an explicit, versioned contract between managed workloads and the host kernel. Its earlier phase as an independent Rust kernel booting through UEFI is complete, with that source preserved separately while active development continues on the userspace version.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。它作为普通 Linux 进程运行，并在受管理工作负载与主机内核之间建立明确且有版本控制的契约。此前通过 UEFI 启动、以 Rust 编写的独立内核阶段已经结束，相关源代码被单独保留，当前开发转向用户态版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>114</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>76</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50426207" data-id="50426207" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="109" data-comments="40" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207" data-time="1794234840">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ikey4u/zebkit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zebkit – Chromium as an embeddable library</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit——可嵌入的 Chromium 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows. It includes Blink, V8 with its JIT tiers, PDFium, FFmpeg, and other Chrome components, but omits the Chrome browser and Electron runtime. Applications can use it through C, C++, Rust, Python, Go, or JavaScript bindings.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。它包含 Blink、带有各级 JIT 的 V8、PDFium、FFmpeg 及其他 Chrome 组件，但不包含 Chrome 浏览器本身或 Electron 运行时。应用可以通过 C、C++、Rust、Python、Go 或 JavaScript 绑定使用它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>109</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50468944" data-id="50468944" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|AI|Web" data-score="107" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468944" data-time="1794497152">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chocoford/ExcalidrawZ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ: Local Native Excalidraw on macOS/iOS</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ：macOS/iOS 原生本地版 Excalidraw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas. It adds native document management, iCloud sync, history, export, AI assistance, MCP integration, and other Apple-platform features. Drawings are handled as application documents rather than loose browser downloads.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。它增加了原生文件管理、iCloud 同步、历史记录、导出、AI 辅助、MCP 集成及其他 Apple 平台功能。绘图会作为应用文档管理，而不是浏览器中零散下载的文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agweb – a browser for agents that stays out of your way</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Agweb——不妨碍用户操作的代理浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus. Agents control the single window from the shell through an agweb command modeled on Playwright CLI, while users can watch all tabs live in one view. Idle tabs are suspended; the author reports that five open pages used 0% CPU on a Mac, compared with five to seven busy cores from Chrome’s GPU process.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。代理通过类似 Playwright CLI 的 agweb 命令从 shell 控制这个窗口，用户则可以在一个视图中实时查看所有标签页。闲置标签页会被挂起；作者称，在 Mac 上打开五个页面时其 CPU 占用为 0%，而 Chrome 的 GPU 进程会占用五到七个繁忙核心。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>87</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>26</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460263" data-id="50460263" data-tags="Programming" data-score="82" data-comments="14" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263" data-time="1794438567">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jjba23/sift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sift: LSP, Lint, Format Lisp, Scheme and Emacs Lisp</span><span class='zh'>Sift：用于 Lisp、Scheme 和 Emacs Lisp 的 LSP、Lint 与格式化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp. It reports malformed Lisp source and rewrites whitespace while preserving comments and read-time forms in their original positions. Formatting an already formatted file produces no further changes, a property asserted across the test suite.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。它会报告格式错误的 Lisp 源码并重写空白，同时保留注释和读取时形式的原始位置。对已经格式化的文件再次格式化不会产生变化，该特性在整个测试套件中都有断言验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>82</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>14</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50416640" data-id="50416640" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="76" data-comments="17" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50416640" data-time="1794168606">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/popvax/yash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cancer vaccine for Yash, he&#x27;s diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer</span><span class='zh'>Yash 的癌症疫苗：他被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry. Yashdeep Bindal was diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer in January 2025 at age 32, shortly after returning to work following the birth of his daughter Maya.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。Yashdeep Bindal 于 2025 年 1 月、32 岁时被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌；此前他刚在女儿 Maya 出生后重返工作岗位。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FURSOY/fursoy-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Fursoy – Out-of-order RV64IMAFDV core in SystemVerilog (Linux 6.6)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 SystemVerilog 编写、支持乱序执行的 RV64IMAFDV 核心（Linux 6.6）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog. It supports RV64IMAFDV with Zba and Zbb extensions, boots mainline Linux 6.6.50 and BusyBox under Sv39 virtual memory, and includes separate machine, supervisor, and user privilege levels. Synthesis in Nangate 45nm reached 500 MHz timing closure across eight core functional blocks.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。它支持带 Zba 和 Zbb 扩展的 RV64IMAFDV，可在 Sv39 虚拟内存下启动主线 Linux 6.6.50 和 BusyBox，并具备机器、监管者和用户三级权限。该设计在 Nangate 45nm 工艺中完成了八个核心功能模块的 500 MHz 时序收敛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/klad-app/klad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Klad, a paint app your coding agent can drive (source-available)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Klad，一款可由编码代理驱动的绘画应用（源代码可用）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans. It supports brush painting, frame-by-frame animation, bones and inverse kinematics, vectors, text layers, colors, layer effects, and persistent unlimited undo. Its editable strokes are intended to let agents create drafts that people can refine stroke by stroke.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。它支持笔刷绘画、逐帧动画、骨骼与反向运动学、矢量、文本图层、颜色、图层效果，以及可跨文件重新打开保留的无限撤销。它将可编辑笔触作为核心，使代理能够生成草稿，再由人类逐笔修改。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/basetenlabs/deepseek-ultrafast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware. It supports a 1-million-token context length and multi-server KV-cache sharing. The project measures decode speed, step time, and acceptance length using a Tetris-generation prompt.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。它支持 100 万 token 的上下文长度和多服务器 KV 缓存共享。项目使用生成俄罗斯方块的提示词测量解码速度、步骤耗时和接受长度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eclipse-mosquitto/mosquitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto – An open source MQTT broker</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto：开源 MQTT 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1. It also provides C and C++ client libraries and command-line tools for publishing, subscribing, and administering the broker.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。它还提供 C 和 C++ 客户端库，以及用于发布、订阅和管理代理的命令行工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/Micron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Micron – Oberon with the Power of C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Micron——带有 C 能力的 Oberon</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability. Its goal is to use one language for boot code, device drivers, operating-system kernels, and ordinary applications without a garbage collector. It supports explicit memory layouts, signed and unsigned integers, pointer operations, and direct C interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。它的目标是用同一种语言编写启动代码、设备驱动、操作系统内核和普通应用，同时不依赖垃圾回收器。该语言支持显式内存布局、有符号和无符号整数、指针操作以及直接的 C 互操作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>53</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413266' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50503930" data-id="50503930" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web" data-score="47" data-comments="9" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503930" data-time="1794715751">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Tthanh2k6/turbo-browser-mcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Turbo-Browser-MCP – Batch forms, DOM deltas and submit guards for MCP</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Turbo-Browser-MCP——为 MCP 提供批量表单、DOM 增量和提交保护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation. It combines 25–33 fine-grained browser primitives into 10 batched tools, with delta DOM snapshots, React/Shadow DOM/iframe form filling, direct disk uploads, parallel tabs, and network-blocking presets. It also includes a default human-in-the-loop guard before form submission.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。它将 25 至 33 个细粒度浏览器原语整合为 10 个批量工具，并支持 DOM 增量快照、React/Shadow DOM/iframe 表单填写、直接从磁盘上传文件、并行标签页和网络阻断预设。提交表单前，它还默认提供人工介入保护。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>47</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503930' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>9</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50443264" data-id="50443264" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Data|DevOps" data-score="46" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264" data-time="1794330969">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTSLM/TimeNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TimeNet: Open-source data infrastructure for Temporal Foundation Models</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet：面向时间基础模型的开源数据基础设施</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models. It supports tasks including classification, question answering, scalar prediction, temporal localization, forecasting, generation, editing, and correspondence. The project reports that training on multiple temporal datasets improved average F1 performance by 14% for OpenTSLM and 22% for SLIP.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。它支持分类、问答、标量预测、时间定位、预测、生成、编辑和对应关系等任务。项目方称，在多个时间数据集上训练后，OpenTSLM 的平均 F1 性能提升了 14%，SLIP 提升了 22%。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>46</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50429144" data-id="50429144" data-tags="Show HN|AI|DevOps" data-score="40" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429144" data-time="1794247544">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Dodaios/ste80-humanizer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: STE80 Humanizer – de-AI text by writing 80% of the way to ASD-STE100</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer：将 AI 文本改写至接近 ASD-STE100 的 80%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning. It targets roughly 80% compliance with ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English. The example focuses on Kubernetes liveness probes and retains both their restart behavior and the warning that misconfiguration can cause cascading restarts.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。它的目标是让文本达到约 80% 的 ASD-STE100 简明技术英语规范。示例内容围绕 Kubernetes 存活探针展开，并保留了探针触发重启的行为以及配置错误可能导致级联重启的警告。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/no382001/zipc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A ZIP-style Prolog VM for trilog</span><span class='zh'>用于 trilog 的 ZIP 风格 Prolog 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C. Its goal is to provide enough of a VM to compare it with the current trilog runtime. ZIP is the abstract machine described in the 1984 book Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。其目标是先搭建足够完整的虚拟机，以便与当前的 trilog 运行时进行比较。ZIP 是 1984 年《Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine》中描述的抽象机器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/db-branch/db-branch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: db-branch – database branches in seconds for AI agents and CI, via ZFS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：db-branch——通过 ZFS 为 AI 代理和 CI 秒速创建数据库分支</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones. The self-hosted tool can provide isolated databases for migrations, AI agents, or pull requests without copying the underlying data. Branches can be reset or discarded, and the project is licensed under Apache-2.0.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。这款自托管工具无需复制底层数据，就能为迁移、AI 代理或每个拉取请求提供独立数据库。分支可以重置或丢弃，项目采用 Apache-2.0 许可证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Christ10-8/reflota" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Reflota – Open-source data recovery for NTFS/FAT disks with phased copy</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Reflota——支持分阶段复制的 NTFS/FAT 开源数据恢复工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files. It reads disks directly: for NTFS, it reconstructs the folder tree from the master file table and directory metadata, while for FAT and exFAT it reads directories and follows cluster chains. It is intended for cases where Windows reports that a disk is unformatted, a folder will not open, or Explorer hangs.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。它会直接读取磁盘：处理 NTFS 时读取主文件表并根据目录元数据重建文件夹树，处理 FAT 和 exFAT 时读取目录并跟踪簇链。当 Windows 报告磁盘未格式化、文件夹无法打开或资源管理器卡死时，该工具可用于尝试恢复数据。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>30</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50482455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50413970" data-id="50413970" data-tags="Show HN|AI" data-score="22" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413970" data-time="1794150727">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sshah03/recalc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Recalc – Spreadsheet where cells can be Claude Code or Codex terminals</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Recalc——单元格可以运行 Claude Code 或 Codex 的电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session. Sessions can communicate through grid cells, named cells, or an MCP server, and can be continued in a regular terminal. The tool currently runs locally on macOS, with Linux support described as less tested.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。不同会话可以通过网格单元格、命名单元格或 MCP 服务器互相通信，也可以在普通终端中继续运行。目前该工具可在 macOS 本地运行，Linux 支持则尚未充分测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Neighborhood-Grid/flatpoints" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: 3D to 2D, point clouds flattening (geometry images)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：3D 转 2D，展平点云（几何图像）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds. It maps each 3D point from a point cloud sampled on an unknown surface to a 2D pixel, arranging the points into a square grid. The resulting flat representation can serve as an image-like indexing structure.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。它把从未知曲面采样得到的每个三维点映射为二维像素，并将这些点排列到方形网格中。生成的平面表示可以作为类似图像的索引结构。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WolveKafka – Consume Kafka with a plain HTTP endpoint</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：WolveKafka——通过纯 HTTP 接口消费 Kafka</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language. It manages consumer groups, batching, retries, offsets, and dead-letter queues. The project includes a quickstart, examples, deployment guidance, and comparison material.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。它负责管理消费者组、批处理、重试、偏移量和死信队列。项目还提供快速入门、示例、部署指南和对比资料。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/agda/agda/releases/tag/v2.9.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agda v2.9.0</span><span class='zh'>Agda v2.9.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode. The release also includes substantial performance improvements and drops support for GHC 8.8, 8.10, and 9.0. Agda is a dependently typed programming language and interactive theorem prover.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。该版本还带来了显著的性能改进，并停止支持 GHC 8.8、8.10 和 9.0。Agda 是一种依赖类型编程语言和交互式定理证明器。</span></p>
